Lately, I've been waking up with this... this heavy, gray feeling in my chest. It's hard to explain, but even when my apartment is totally quiet and nothing bad is actually happening, I just feel so down. Like, I feel like I could just break down and cry over something as stupid as a coffee mug left in the sink.\n\nDoctor: How long do these periods of feeling sad or depressed usually last?\n\nPatient: Well, they can drag on for a few weeks at a time once they start. But overall, these heavier depressive stretches have really been showing up since my separation... which was about 11 months ago. It's been happening since then, even before this latest move made everything feel a lot sharper.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your really energetic and productive periods are separate from your normal self, like they're distinct episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... I'm not really sure. I haven't really thought of it like that. It doesn't necessarily feel like a completely separate episode or... like I'm a different person, if that's what you mean. So, I guess I wouldn't say I've noticed them being totally distinct from my normal self.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you can control when these really energetic and productive periods start and stop?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I can control it at all.
